性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和

2026年 11月 20日 全6頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 10月全国消費者物価 
電気代などエネルギーが上昇に転じ、コアCPIは伸び率拡大

経済調査部 エコノミスト 横田 凱

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 10月の全国コア CPI（除く生鮮食品）は前年比＋2.2％と前月から伸び率が拡
大した。資源高が波及し電気代やガス代が大きく上昇したことでエネルギーの前年比
伸び率がプラスに転じた。一方、生鮮食品やエネルギーを除いた全国新コアコア CPI
は同＋2.2％と前月から伸び率が縮小した。コメ価格の下落の影響で非耐久消費財（除
く生鮮食品・エネルギー）の伸びが減速したことが主因だ。 
◼ コア CPIの前年比の動きを財・サービス別に見ると、エネルギーの伸び率がプラスに
転じたほか、耐久消費財の伸び率が拡大した。非耐久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネル
ギー）、サービスの伸び率は縮小した。半耐久消費財の伸び率は横ばいだった。 
◼ 先行きの物価上昇率について、コア CPI上昇率は 26年度末にかけて前年比＋3％超ま
で高まると見込んでいる。原油高が、エネルギーだけでなく、幅広い財・サービスの
価格へ波及するとみられる。賃上げの価格転嫁と併せ、基調的な物価への上昇圧力と
なるだろう。27 年度は、こうした物価上昇圧力は継続する一方で、飲食料品にかかる
消費税率の引き下げにより、コアCPIの上昇率は縮小するとみられる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 6

26年10月 CPI：エネルギーが再びプラスになり、コア CPI上昇率は拡大 
2026年 10月の全国コア CPI（除く生鮮食品）は前年比＋2.2％と前月から伸び率が拡大した
（図表1）。3-5カ月前の原油価格等を参照する電気代やガス代が大きく上昇し、政策による押し
下げ効果を上回ったことで、エネルギー（9月：同▲0.6％→10月：同＋2.2％）が 3カ月ぶり
にプラスに転じたことが主因だ。なお、政府のエネルギー高対策による押し下げの効果は依然
として大きく、政策要因1を除いたコアCPIの伸び率は当社の試算で同＋3.6％程度である2。 
一方、全国新コアコア CPI（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）（前年比＋2.2％）の伸び率は前月
から縮小した。コメ価格の下落などにより、生鮮食品を除く食料の伸びが減速したことで、非
耐久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）の伸び率が縮小した。 
図表1：消費者物価指数（前年比、％）

図表2：全国 CPIの水準（季節調整値）3

1 当社の試算においては、燃料油の激変緩和措置、電気・ガス料金支援、高等学校授業料の実質無償化、公立
小・中学校の給食費無償化、水道料減免を指す。 
2 日本銀行による特殊要因を除いたコア CPIの試算値は、2026年 11月25日 14時頃の公表が見込まれる。 
3 2025年基準の固定基準年方式によるもの。同基準のラスパイレス連鎖基準方式による指数は、2027年1月分
より公開される（2027年2月19日予定）。

### Page 3

1

3 / 6

原油高が反映され電気代や都市ガス代が上昇し、エネルギーの伸び率がプラスに転換 
コア CPIの前年比の動きを財・サービス別に見ると（図表 3）、エネルギー（前年比＋2.2％）
の伸び率がプラスに転じたほか、耐久消費財（同＋4.1％）の伸び率が拡大した。一方、非耐
久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）（同＋2.8％）やサービス（同＋1.4％）の伸び率が縮
小した。また、半耐久消費財（同＋3.1％）の伸び率は前月から横ばいだった。 
エネルギーに含まれる 5品目はいずれも前月から伸び率が拡大した。とりわけ、11カ月ぶり
にプラスに転じた電気代（9月：前年比▲2.4％→10月：同＋1.6％）や、都市ガス代（9月：
同＋0.2％→10月：同＋5.3％）の寄与度が大きい。それぞれ、政府による補助額は前年同月よ
り多かったものの、今年夏ごろの資源高を反映した価格の上昇がそれを上回った。 
耐久消費財では、携帯電話機（9月：前年比＋10.9％→10月：同＋17.2％）やパソコン（ノ
ート型）（9月：同＋0.8％→10月：同＋2.7％）など、電子機器の伸び率が拡大した。 
一方、非耐久消費財（除く生鮮食品、エネルギー）では、うるち米Ｂ（9 月：前年比▲
21.1％→10月：同▲29.8％）やうるち米Ａ（9月：同▲19.6％→10月：同▲29.4％）など、コ
メ価格を中心に一部の食料品の下落幅が拡大した。 
サービスでは、通信料（携帯電話）（9月：前年比＋1.6％→10月：同＋0.4％）や自動車保険
料（任意）（9月：同＋5.9％→10月：同＋4.6％）などで、前年同月の値上げの裏の影響が表れ
たことなどから伸び率が縮小した。 
また、半耐久消費財では、マット（9月：前年比▲0.0％→10月：同＋3.5％）や女性用セー
ター（長袖）（9月：同▲0.2％→10月：同＋2.7％）などの伸び率がプラスに転じた一方、自動
車タイヤ（9月：同＋1.4％→10 月：同＋0.4％）やメモリーカード（9月：同＋152.0％→10
月：同＋107.9％）などの伸び率が縮小した。 
図表3：全国コア CPIの前年比と寄与度（左）、全国コア CPIの内訳（右）

4 / 6

先行き：原油高などの波及で、コア CPIは26年度末にかけて＋3％超を予想 
先行きの物価上昇率について、コア CPI上昇率は 26年度末にかけて前年比＋3％超まで高ま
るとみている。当面は、原油高の幅広い財・サービスへの波及が物価を押し上げる主因となる
だろう。また、賃金と物価の循環的な上昇も続くとみられる。 
原油価格の動向には注意が必要だ。米国・イスラエルとイランの間で断続的な攻撃の応酬は
続いており、中東の和平に向けた先行きはいまだ不透明である。原油価格の代表的な指標であ
る WTI先物価格は 11月 19日終値で 83ドル/バレル程度と、中東情勢の悪化前よりも高い状況
が続いている。 
足元の原油高は、直接的にエネルギー価格を押し上げる。電気・ガスの燃料費調整制度で
は、3-5カ月前のエネルギー資源の平均輸入価格が料金に反映されるため、原油高がラグを伴
って波及し、前年比での影響は長く続く。関連して、政府は 26年 12月から 27年2月使用分
（請求は翌月）に電気・ガス料金支援を再開する4。しかし、26年1-3月使用分にも補助を行っ
ていたため、前年比でみた物価上昇率の抑制効果は限定的なものとなるだろう（巻末図表「エ
ネルギー価格のコアCPIへの影響とその見通し」）。 
また、原油高は、エネルギー価格だけでなく、輸送費や原材料費の増加を通じて幅広い品目
の価格にも波及するため5、基調的な物価への上昇圧力となるとみられる。さらに、ドル円レー
トは足元で 150円/ドル台後半から 160円/ドル程度と、前年比で円安水準にある。原油価格の
上昇と併せて輸入物価への押し上げ圧力が強い状態であり、消費者物価への波及が見込まれ
る。 
賃上げによる人件費の増加分を販売価格に転嫁する動きも継続するだろう。26年度春闘にお
ける定期昇給相当込みの賃上げ率は、加重平均で5.01％と高水準を維持している6。さらに、26
年度の最低賃金の全国加重平均額も前年度比 5％程度引き上げられた7。こうした賃金上昇は、
サービス価格を中心に物価を押し上げると見込んでいる。 
これらの物価上昇圧力は27年度にかけても継続するだろう。他方、国会で審議中の飲食料
品の消費減税8が法案通り実施されると、コア CPI上昇率は前年比1.2％pt程度押し下げられる
ことから、27年度後半には前年比＋1％台まで縮小するだろう。

4 詳細は資源エネルギー庁「電気・ガス料金支援」（2026年11月20日閲覧）を参照。 
5 中村華奈子「原油高の国内への波及経路と価格転嫁率を踏まえた消費者物価への影響」（大和総研レポー
ト、2026年4月17日） 
6 日本労働組合総連合会（連合）「3年連続の 5％超え！ ～2026春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結
果について～」（2026年7月3日） 
7 厚生労働省「令和 8年度 地域別最低賃金 答申状況」（2026年9月3日） 
8 国会で審議中の「租税及び社会保険料による負担の軽減のための飲食料品に係る消費税率の臨時的な引下げ
及び就業者負担軽減支援金の支給に関する法律」が成立すれば、軽減税率対象の飲食料品の消費税率は 2027
年 4月1日から2年間、現在の 8％から 1％へと引き下げられる。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國 10 月財政赤字大幅擴張、房市買氣探底，通膨壓力仍具韌性 
經濟 
⚫ 美國 2027 財年首月財政赤字達 4,040.6 億美元，年增 42.1%。當月總收
入 3,689.4 億美元（年減 8.8%），總支出 7,730 億美元（年增 12.2%），
截至 10 月底公債總額升至 40.52 兆美元，財政壓力顯著加劇。 
⚫ 受房貸利率攀升至 7.33%影響，2026 年 10 月美國成屋銷售年率跌至 375
萬戶的 16 年新低。目前房價中位數微升至 41.67 萬美元，待售庫存增
至 159 萬戶，房市呈現「量縮、價微揚、庫存增」的態勢。考量目前就
業市場仍持續成長，一旦利率開始回落，預期將能有效釋放市場積壓的
買氣。 
⚫ 10 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%，低於上月 0.6%，惟高於市場
預期；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%持平。住房、食品及能源價格續升，其中住
房貢獻逾三成，顯示通膨壓力仍存；惟受 2025 年 10 月政府停擺影響，
10 月年增率未發布。 
⚫ 10 月美國 PPI 月增 0.3%，較上月 1.4%明顯放緩，但年增率由 6.3%上
升至 6.5%，創 2022 年 11 月以來新高。能源與服務價格漲勢降溫，惟
食品、非能源商品及貨運價格續揚，顯示生產端通膨壓力仍高，後續須
關注成本轉嫁及對 Fed 利率政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 2026 年 APEC 峰會將於 11 月 18 日至 19 日在中國深圳舉行。會前中美
相繼延長部分關稅豁免及貿易限制暫停措施至 2027 年 1 月，中國暫停
24%對美加徵關稅，惟保留 10%稅率，美國則延長 178 項中國商品關稅
豁免，釋出經貿緩和訊號，有助降低供應鏈不確定性。惟雙方結構性分
歧仍存，後續須關注川習會談成果及貿易政策走向。 
⚫ 輝達創辦人黃仁勳因婚外情及涉國安爭議引發調查，於 11 月 12 日辭去
執行長及董事職務，結束 33 年掌舵生涯，由財務長克雷斯暫代執行長。
消息重挫輝達股價及市值，並引發市場對全球 AI 產業、供應鏈及公司
治理風險的高度關注。 
短評 
⚫ 美國 10 月財政赤字顯著擴大，反映歲入下滑及政府支出負擔加重；成
屋銷售創多年低點，顯示高利率持續壓抑房市需求。儘管 CPI 及 PPI 月
增幅放緩，惟核心通膨及生產端價格壓力仍頑強，顯示物價上行風險尚

中 
國 
國際能源價格上漲抑制貿易順差擴張 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月出口年增率較 9 月續增 1.6 個百分點至 37.9%，出口總值為 4,123
億美元；進口年增率增 10.6 個百分點至 41.6%，進口總值為 3,071 億美
元。 
⚫ 10 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 9 月的 5%升至 5.7%；但消費者通膨(CPI)、
核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別回落 0.2 個百分點為 0.7%、0.8%。 
短評 
⚫ 由於國際能源價格上漲，進口增速高於出口，並抑制貿易順差擴張、減
緩外匯存底流入速度，惟此亦有助於減緩人民幣升值速度。 
⚫ 在民間消費乏力之下，企業仍大多自行吸收成本，加上政府控制零售燃
油及民生水電價格漲幅，故目前原油價格高漲僅少數反映於消費端。

一、 美國—10 月財政赤字大幅擴張、房市買氣探底，通膨壓力仍具韌性 
2026 年 10 月(2027 財年第 1 個月)聯邦政府財政赤字達 4,040.6 億美元，較去
年同期的 2,843.3 億美元大幅擴大 42.1% 。當月政府總收入為 3,689.4 億美元，年
減 8.8%，主因個人所得稅淨收入由去年同期的 2,169.5 億美元下滑至 1,863.8 億
美元，且 10 月關稅退稅高達 67.3 億美元(遠高於去年同期的 17.4 億美元)，大幅
抵銷了 259.5 億美元的關稅總收入，致使關稅淨收入雖較 9 月的 59.1 億美元回升
至 192.2 億美元，仍較去年同期的 313.5 億美元大減 38.7%。支出方面，10 月政
府總支出達 7,730.0 億美元，年增 12.2%，除因 11 月 1 日適逢非營業日，導致部
分現役與退役軍人薪資、退伍軍人福利及醫療保險等款項提前至 10 月撥付外，
醫療保險(1,700.5 億美元)、社會安全(1,426.9 億美元)及國防(1,288.8 億美元)等主
要項目均較去年同期成長；同時，10 月聯邦淨利息支出攀升至 1,030.8 億美元，
年增 13.4%。整體而言，在歲入下滑與福利、國防及利息支出擴張的雙重影響下，
美國新財年開局赤字顯著走高，截至 10 月底聯邦公債總額已進一步推升至 40.52
兆美元(其中公眾持有債務為 32.74 兆美元)，龐大債務規模與後續利息負擔仍將
是牽動美國財政穩健度的核心變數。 
2026 年 10 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率降至 375 萬戶，較上月減少
5.5%，較去年同期下滑 8.8%，創下自 2010 年 8 月以來的最低水準，主因是 30 年
期固定房貸平均利率攀升至 7.33%(高於上月的 6.86%及去年同期的 6.25%)，使購
屋負擔加重，全美住房可負擔指數亦由去年同期的 107.3 降至 100.7。觀察區域表
現，10 月西部、南部及中西部銷量分別月減 9.7%、6.5%及 3.2%，僅東北部維持
持平，且四大地區銷量均較去年同期衰退。儘管買氣受抑，全美成屋售價中位數
仍年增 0.4%至 41.67 萬美元，寫下連續 40 個月年增紀錄；供給端則持續擴增，
待售庫存升至 159 萬戶，月增 1.3%、年增 4.6%，相當於 5.1 個月供給量(高於上
月的 4.7 個月與去年同期的 4.4 個月)。全美房地產經紀人協會(NAR)首席經濟學
家 Lawrence Yun 指出，在高利率衝擊下，10 月成屋市場呈現「量縮、價微揚、
庫存增」的格局，但全國 8,800 萬名屋主整體財務狀況依然穩健，不良資產交易
僅占 2%，考量目前就業市場仍持續成長，未來一旦房貸利率回落，市場可望釋

出可觀的積壓買氣。 
2026 年 10 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.4%，低於上月之
0.6%，惟高於市場預期的 0.3%；扣除食品與能源價格之核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，與
前月持平，並高於市場預期的 0.2%。在細項方面，住房指數月增 0.3%，貢獻整
體 CPI 月增幅逾三成，仍為推升通膨的重要因素；食品指數月增 0.5%，其中家庭
食品及外食價格分別上漲 0.4%及 0.5%；能源指數月增 0.7%，其中汽油價格及電
價分別上漲 1.0%及 0.9%，惟汽油漲幅較上月明顯放緩。核心項目方面，休閒娛
樂、醫療照護、汽車保險、家庭用品及教育等價格持續上揚，二手車及卡車價格
則下跌 1.2%，通訊價格亦小幅下降。另受 2025 年 10 月美國政府停擺影響，當月
未進行 CPI 調查並缺乏比較基期資料，美國勞工局未發布 2026 年 10 月整體 CPI
及多數主要指數之年增率。整體而言，10 月美國物價仍受住房、食品及能源價格
上漲帶動，核心 CPI 月增率則連續第三個月維持 0.3%，顯示服務類通膨壓力仍具
韌性，後續通膨走勢仍須持續觀察能源價格及住房成本變化。 
2026 年 10 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 0.3%，明顯低於上
月修正後之 1.4%，惟年增率由 6.3%升至 6.5%，創 2022 年 11 月以來最大漲幅，
顯示生產端價格壓力仍高。其中，最終需求商品價格月增 0.5%，低於上月之 2.5%，
逾六成漲幅來自不含食品及能源之商品價格上升，其中基本有機化學品價格上漲
3.9%；食品價格亦月增 1.3%，惟能源價格微降 0.1%，其中柴油價格下跌 7.9%。
另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.2%，低於上月之 1.0%，主要受運輸及倉儲
服務價格上漲 2.0%帶動，其中貨運卡車運輸價格上漲 3.9%，貢獻服務價格漲幅
逾八成，惟貿易服務價格下降 0.2%，抵銷部分漲幅。剔除食品、能源及貿易服務
後之核心最終需求 PPI 月增 0.5%，與上月持平，年增率則由 5.0%小幅降至 4.9%。
整體而言，10 月美國 PPI 月增幅明顯放緩，主要反映能源價格回落及服務價格漲
勢降溫，惟食品、部分非能源商品及貨運價格仍持續上漲，加上整體 PPI 年增率
進一步攀升，顯示生產端通膨壓力仍高，後續須關注企業成本轉嫁情形、能源及
運輸價格變化，以及對消費者物價與聯準會利率政策之影響。 
2026 年亞太經濟合作組織(APEC)領袖峰會將於 11 月 18 日至 19 日在中國深
圳舉行。在峰會召開前及「川習三會」前夕，中美雙方相繼延長部分關稅豁免與
暫停措施，釋出緩和貿易緊張關係的訊號，並為後續經貿協商創造較有利條件。
中國宣布自 11 月 10 日起續暫停實施 24%的對美加徵關稅兩個月，並保留 10%稅

輝達(NVIDIA)創辦人黃仁勳近日在被爆出與疑涉中國情報背景的女子黃樂
(Alice Huang)有婚外情並於 11 月 8 日坦承對妻子不忠致歉後，因外部與董事會調
查持續延燒，且《紐約時報》與《路透社》接連揭露其與直屬下屬輝達醫療保健
副總裁金伯莉·鮑爾(Kimberly Powell)及另一名內部女員工也存在婚外情，最終於
11 月 12 日正式宣布辭去總裁、執行長與董事會職位，結束其掌舵輝達 33 年的傳
奇生涯，並由財務長克雷斯(Colette Kress)暫代執行長兵符，同時任命哈維·瓊斯
(Harvey C. Jones)為首任董事會主席。同時，美國總統川普於 11 月 9 日證實聯邦
調查局(FBI)已介入調查該起事件及潛在的反情報國安疑慮，並表示雖與黃仁勳通
話且相信其不知女方背景的說法，仍會交由情報部門進一步核實。黃仁勳閃辭的
消息隨即震撼全球科技與金融市場，導致輝達股價在 11 月 12 日開盤後盤中一度
重挫逾 14%、單日市值一度蒸發約 6,000 億美元，不僅重創高「含輝量」台股 ETF
表現，更引發外界對全球 AI 產業版圖及美中科技供應鏈穩定性的高度憂慮。儘
管黃仁勳矽谷盟友與部分員工近期發起連署與遊說，試圖推動他重返輝達，但美
國財政部長貝森特(Scott Bessent)已公開呼籲輝達領導層應進行「大幅改組」，使
後續接班與公司治理動向備受矚目。 
二、歐元區—勞動市場開始出現疲態 
 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率二次估計值維持 0.4%不變，顯示在地緣政治不確
定性升高、能源價格再度上揚的環境下，整體景氣仍具支撐力；今年前三季經濟
成長率(年化率)則大約為 1.1%，大致持平去年水準。從需求面來看，政府支出與
民間消費為主要成長動能，其中民間消費受惠於較高的儲蓄水準及穩定的就業市
場，減輕能源價格上漲的衝擊，並且電動車、油電混合車需求持續增加，抵銷利
率升高及地緣政治波動對投資帶來的負面影響。

### Page 8

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

7

展望未來，從歐元區採購經理人指標(PMI)顯示第四季經濟仍可望延續溫和擴
張，在能源衝擊逐漸消退後，2027 年成長動能有望進一步增強。不過，第三季就
業呈現零成長，主要是德、法等核心國家就業疲弱，抵銷南歐國家就業增加的影
響，勞動市場可能逐漸走疲為未來復甦的隱憂。若就業狀況不如預期，恐進一步
抑制薪資表現，並削弱消費信心，進而影響經濟復甦。 
三、日本—經濟成長停滯，內外需動能轉弱 
 2026 年第三季經濟成長季增率持平，結束連續三季的經濟擴張，主要受通膨、
部分地區自然災害及全球需求疲弱影響，導致私人消費季減 0.3%，出口亦下滑
0.2%，加上進口反彈 2.5%，使淨貿易拖累 GDP 達 0.6 個百分點；惟企業投資季
增 1.6%，為近三季以來首次成長，對景氣形成一定支撐。整體而言，日本內外需
動能均有所轉弱，後續民間消費回溫及企業投資能否持續，將是觀察經濟能否恢
復成長的重要指標。 
四、中國—國際能源價格上漲抑制貿易順差擴張 
10 月出口年增率較 9 月續增 1.6 個百分點至 37.9%，出口總值為 4,123 億美
元；累計前十個月出口年增率上升為 22.7%，累積出口總值為 37,827 億美元。前
十個月出口主要成長項目(累積年增率)包含 IC(111.1%)、自動數據處理設備
(60.6%)、船舶(27.7%)、汽車(52.2%)，其中 IC 及自動數據處理設備出口加速成長，
為 10 月出口年增率加速上升的主要因素。 
10 月進口年增率較 9 月增 10.6 個百分點至 41.6%，進口總值為 3,071 億美
元；累計前十個月進口值成長 29%至 27,401 億美元。該月原油、成品油進口金額
大幅增加，為帶動進口加速成長的主要因素；IC、自動數據處理設備(包含伺服器
等)進口分別成長 65.1%、136.7%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部分伺服器
關鍵組件有賴於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前十個月貿易順差 10,426 億美
元，僅較去年同期增加 8.7%，增幅明顯低於出口，顯示出口擴張同時伴隨關鍵原
物料進口大幅增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價值產品依賴
國際供應鏈支持。此外，因貿易順差增幅有限，10 月外匯存底僅增 49 億美元至
約 3.41 兆美元。 
10 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 9 月的 5%升至 5.7%，主因原油價格居高不下，
燃料購進成本持續上漲，提高生產成本所致。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格

用相關內容。

8

年增率續揚 10.5 個百分點至 35%，並帶動燃料加工業出廠價格年增率上揚至
29.9%，此外，對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學品、化纖出廠價格年增率加速升
至 16.4%、16%，顯示原油價格上漲的影響開始擴散至中下游產業。此外，由於
近期鋼鐵價格走弱，黑色金屬礦產開採出廠價格年減 6.4%，為主要拖累因素。 
10 月消費者通膨(CPI)、核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別回落 0.2 個百分點為
0.7%、0.8%。儘管燃油通膨續增 5.3 個百分點至 20.2%，但食品、房租、汽車、
旅遊價格漲幅皆有放緩，其他商品及服務價格則大致平穩，使消費者通膨較前月
下滑。整體而言，雖然企業生產成本持續上升，但在民間消費乏力之下，企業仍
大多自行吸收成本，加上政府控制零售燃油及民生水電價格漲幅，故目前原油價
格高漲僅少數反映於消費端。惟考量能源成本傳導存在時間差，若油價維持高檔
或進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。 
五、印度及東南亞—印度通膨超標；印尼、菲律賓第三季 GDP 分別達 5.5%及
2.1% 
(1)印度：10 月消費者物價指數（CPI）年增率進一步上揚至 6.09%，遠高於 9 月
的 5.54%，其中食品通膨加速上升至 8.5%，反映聖嬰現象導致季風降雨量低於預
期，影響農作物收成，同時核心通膨率（剔除汽油、柴油和貴金屬）從 9 月的 3.2%
上升至 3.8%，顯示潛在通膨持續走強並有所擴大。由於通膨率高於預期且突破央
行 6%的目標區間上限，預期印度央行 12 月的政策會議將有進一步升息的壓力。 
(2)印尼：2026 年第三季經濟成長率(GDP)年增 5.47%，私人消費成長 5.04%，主
要因政府採取措施，協助家庭抵禦通膨壓力和更高的借貸成本，惟受能源補貼負
擔加重導致財政空間收窄，政府支出大幅放緩至 8.95%，遠低於上季的 15.97%；
固定資產投資成長略放緩至 6.06%；另受石油和能源成本上漲影響，進口成長率
（7.31%）高於出口成長率（4.59%），致淨出口產生負貢獻，惟較上季對經濟的
拖累有所減緩。按產業別觀之，製造業(5.87%)、住宿業（11.22%）、運輸/倉儲業
（8.03%）和通訊業（7.13%）均有所成長，採礦業產出由負轉正 0.9%，抵銷農業
產出受聖嬰現象影響而大幅下降至 0.5%的影響。整體而言，第三季經濟成長優於
預期，惟在財政和貨幣政策收緊的背景下，國內需求可能進一步放緩。 
(3)菲律賓：2026 年第三季經濟成長率(GDP)年增 2.1%，為 2009 年第四季以來（不
計新冠疫情影響）最疲軟的經濟成長，低於政府先前已下調的全年 3.5%至 4.5%

用相關內容。

9

的目標，聖嬰現象令農業產量低迷和能源成本高漲持續抑制經濟活動。家庭消費
成長放緩至 2.4%，政府支出成長降至 6.8%，固定投資雖小幅復甦仍萎縮 4.4%，
所幸出口成長 8.6%、進口成長放緩至 7.7%，致淨出口呈正向貢獻。按產業別觀
之，工業成長加快至 5.4%，服務業小幅放緩至 9%，惟農業產出受氣候影響而衰
退 0.4%。整體而言，第三季經濟活動主要受氣候干擾，農業、服務產出下滑並影
響消費支出，固定投資在公共建設投資帶動下有好轉跡象，然而央行升息提高借
貸成本，對私人投資產構成壓力，隨極端天氣事件的影響逐漸消退、家庭在年底
假期前消費意願提升、以及政府加快基礎設施建設，將有助於第四季經濟加快復
甦。 
六、台灣—AI 商機持續發酵，出口動能強勁 
10 月出口達 967.6 億美元，年增 58.9%，創歷史新高，主要由 AI 及高效能運
算需求帶動，其中資通與視聽產品、電子零組件合計約占總出口 83%，顯示 AI 伺
服器、半導體及相關零組件已成為出口成長核心。同時，對美出口達 329.3 億美
元、年增 55.9%，東協亦大幅成長 83.8%，反映 AI 需求及全球供應鏈重組正帶動
出口市場結構改變。惟進口年增 75.1%，增幅高於出口，後續宜觀察進口增加是
否有效轉化為產能及出口，以及 AI 需求、高基期與全球科技業資本支出變化對
出口動能的影響。

鴻海 (2317 TT) 
規模效益顯現

永豐觀點 
AI 伺服器為成長主軸，規模效益顯現，觀察政策長期對 AI 資料中心建置的影響。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持鴻海買進建議：鴻海 3Q26 財報、4Q26 展望優於預期。2027 年維持
以 AI 伺服器為成長主軸，公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求仍大於供給，關鍵
零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業 AI 資料中心建置
時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同時體現獲利率效
益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。研究部預估鴻海 2026 年/2027 年稅後
EPS 19.33 元/25.51 元。維持鴻海買進建議，目標價 356 元(14x2027FEPS)。

ESG 評析 
鴻海企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。

2

鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產
品，以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯
(601138 SH)、富智康(2038 HK)及鴻騰精密(6088 HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研
發資源在電動車、數位醫療、機器人、AI、半導體、5G 等領域。主要客戶為 Apple、
HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、北美四大 CSP 等。3Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
29%、雲端網路 54%、電腦終端 12%、元件及其他 5%。 
3Q26 財報優於預期：鴻海 3Q26 毛利率 6.02%，營益率 3.83%，優於預估的
5.93%、3.77%。鴻海營收規模放大，營運槓桿效益顯現。業外收入貢獻 33 億元，
稅後淨利 775 億元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元，優於預估 5.10 元。3Q26 鴻海存貨金額
1.5 兆，季增 10%，年增 27%，存貨天數 45.7 天，季減 3 天，年減 4 天。存貨狀
況尚屬健康。 
4Q26 展望略優於預期：鴻海展望 4Q26 營運，AI 伺服器出貨量季增高雙位數(部
分專案採 Consignment 商業模式)，預期 ASIC 及 GPU 出貨續增，Vera Rubin AI
伺服器機櫃單價跳升，推估雲端網路營收季增約 15%。消費性產品 iPhone 維持
強勁出貨動能，PC 需求則持續疲弱季減。研究部略為上修 4Q26 營收預估季增
17%，稅後 EPS 上修至 5.99 元。 
AI 伺服器為鴻海營運增長動能 留意長期資料中心建置影響性：因應客戶 AI 建置
需求，鴻海持續支出投入產能，主要客戶為美系大型 CSP、AI 模型商及 Neocloud，
降低資金及庫存風險。並透過與客戶改成採用 Consignment 商業模式，提高營運
資金效率，並優化獲利率表現。研究部預期鴻海維持 Vera Rubin 的主要供應商角
色，整體市占率維持達 40%以上，同時鴻海 2027 年將增加美系 CSP AI ASIC 伺
服器機櫃供應，為 2027 年營運增長動能。公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求
仍大於供給，關鍵零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業
AI 資料中心建置時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同
時體現獲利率效益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。 
綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收 11.2 兆，年增 38%，毛利率 6.0%，營益率
3.7%，稅後淨利 2,710 億，年增 43%，稅後 EPS 19.33 元。2027 年營收 15.5 兆
元，年增 38%，毛利率 5.4%，營益率 3.4%，稅後淨利 3,562 億元，年增 31%，
稅後 EPS 25.51 元。

廣達(2382 TT) Trading Buy

AI 伺服器展望維持不變，投資建議 Trading Buy 
投資建議：廣達目前未見主要 CSP 客戶調整 AI 伺服器訂單，
且考慮到 CSP 對於記憶體、CPU 和 PCB 等關鍵零組件的鎖料
能力，我們預期 Vera Rubin 將於如原先預期於 1Q27 放量，2027
年營運展望維持正向，然而考慮到近期市場對於資料中心建置
的疑慮升溫而可能抑制評價，因此我們給予廣達 Trading Buy 的
投資建議，目標價 324 元。 
3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，稅後 EPS 6.92 元：3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，
QoQ+7.6%；毛利率 4.75%， QoQ-0.27ppts；營業利益 322.04 億
元，QoQ-6.0%；歸屬母公司淨利 284.22 億元，QoQ-0.8%；稅後
EPS 6.92 元。 
PC 市場需求疲弱，伺服器業務支撐營收季增： 3Q26 筆電出貨
960 萬台，QoQ-17%，考慮到筆電零組件漲價帶動 ASP 提高，
我們預估 PC 營收季減高個位數。通用型伺服器營收優於預期，
除 2H26 AI Agent 強勁的需求外，ASP 亦由於零組件漲價而提
升。AI 伺服器部分出貨量較 2Q26 微幅下滑，但同樣受零組件
漲價帶動 ASP 提升影響，預估 AI 伺服器營收仍季增低個位數。 
2027 年伺服器展望不變：近期市場對於限制資料中心相關法案
的擔憂升溫，然而廣達表示目前客戶方面並未調整訂單，就現
階段來看主要 CSP 的資本支出尚未放緩，我們預估 2027 年 AI
伺服器展望將維持不變。我們預期 1Q27 Vera Rubin 將可如先
前預期放量，帶動 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收高雙位數增長。考慮
到 AI Agent 的需求持續成長，預期 2027 年通用型伺服器全年
營收呈雙位數年增。

### Page 2

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 12 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG 
廣達積極推動環境、社會及公司治理（ESG）永續發展，並將節能減碳、人
才培育及供應鏈管理納入企業營運策略。環境方面，公司於 2024 年通過科
學基礎減碳目標倡議（SBTi）審查，承諾以 2022 年為基準，2030 年前將範
疇一及範疇二溫室氣體排放量降低 42%、範疇三降低 25%，並以 2050 年達
成淨零排放為長期目標。公司亦持續推動再生能源使用及能源管理，2024 年
綠電使用占總用電量 47.51%，並設定 2030 年再生能源使用比例達 50%的目
標。 
社會責任方面，廣達重視員工權益、職業安全及人才培育，遵循責任商業聯
盟（ RBA）行為準則，並透過廣達文教基金會及廣藝基金會推動教育與藝文
活動。公司治理方面，廣達持續強化風險管理、資訊安全及供應鏈永續管理，
並參照 TCFD 及 SASB 等國際框架揭露永續資訊，以提升營運透明度及長期
經營韌性。 
公司簡介 
廣達電腦（Quanta Computer Inc.， 2382 TT）成立於 1988 年，總部位於台灣
桃園，為全球主要電子產品原廠設計製造（ODM）廠商之一，主要業務涵蓋
筆記型電腦、伺服器、雲端運算設備及其他智慧電子產品。公司早期以筆記
型電腦代工為主要業務，憑藉產品研發、供應鏈管理及全球製造能力，建立
與國際品牌客戶的長期合作關係，近年則積極擴展雲端運算及人工智慧相
關業務。 
廣達透過旗下雲達科技（QCT）布局資料中心市場，提供伺服器、儲存設備、
網路交換器及整機櫃解決方案，並隨大型雲端服務供應商（CSP）擴 大 AI 基
礎建設投資，逐步深化 AI 伺服器業務布局。相較傳統筆電代工，AI 伺服器
對系統整合、散熱、電源管理及高速互連技術要求較高，帶動產品複雜度及
單機價值提升，成為公司營收成長的重要驅動因素。 
展望未來，廣達持續推進 AI 伺服器及機櫃級系統整合能力，並透過全球生
產據點布局，強化供應鏈韌性及在地化交付能力。隨 AI 運算需求持續擴張，
伺服器業務可望維持公司中長期成長動能，惟仍須關注 CSP 資本支出、產
品轉換時程及新平台量產效率對營運的影響。 
3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，稅後 EPS 7.08 元：3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，QoQ+7.6%，
YoY+125.2%；毛利率 4.75%，QoQ-0.27ppts，YoY-2.10ppts；營業利益 322.04
億元，QoQ-6.0%，YoY+75.3%，營益率 2.89%，QoQ-0.41ppts，YoY-0.82ppts；
歸屬母公司淨利 284.22 億元，QoQ-0.8%， YoY+73.0%；稅後 EPS 7.08 元。
PC 市場需求疲弱，伺服器業務支撐營收季增： 3Q26 筆電出貨 960 萬台，
QoQ-17%， YoY-24%，出貨量略優於 2Q26 預期的 925 萬台，考慮到筆電零
組件漲價帶動 ASP 提高，我們預估 PC 營收季減高個位數。通用型伺服器營
收優於預期，主要原因除 2H26 AI Agent 強勁的需求外，亦由於 ASP 受零
組件漲價影響而提升。AI 伺服器部分出貨量較 2Q26 微幅下滑，但同樣受零
組件漲價帶動 ASP 提升影響，預估 AI 伺服器營收仍季增低個位數。 
零組件漲價及產品組合改變導致毛利率承壓：由於(1)筆電持續因成本上揚
而壓縮獲利，且出貨數下滑不利經濟規模(2)雖 AI 伺服器出貨量季減，但由

### Page 3

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 12 日

3 群益投顧 
ASP 提升驅動營收增長，造成毛利率稀釋(3)通用型伺服器出貨量小幅季增，
且 ASP 提升。綜上原因導致 3Q26 毛利率季減 0.27ppts，帶動營益率季減
0.41ppts，此一情景預估將延續至 2027 年，且我們預估 Vera Rubin 世代平
台 ASP 較 GB300 系列接近倍增，雖營收與獲利將可持續上升，但未來 margin
將持續承壓。雖目前有數個專案採用 Consignment model，然而主要以通用
型伺服器與 ASIC 平台為主，且出貨形式由 L6 至 L11 皆有，營收貢獻相對
於採用 B/S 的整櫃 GPU 伺服器而言佔比極低。據我們了解目前已有確定一
個 Vera Rubin 的大型專案及數個規模較小的專案將採用 Consignment 
model，但預估占整體伺服器營收比重仍低，對於毛利率的改善效果有限。
由於 B/S 將稀釋毛利率，Consignment 則會使營收下滑，因此未來針對 ODM
的獲利能力的評估重點應轉向以營業利益年複合成長率(CAGR)或是 ROIC
為主。 
終止華亞科技園區收購，2028 年產能擴充計畫維持不變：廣達表示，由於
對廠方規格及交付時程等事項無法取得共識，因此撤銷友達華亞科技園區
廠房的收購案，原先規劃的產能將透過海外投資計畫或其他彈性應變方案，
維持 2028 年投產的計畫不變。廣達 3Q26 資本支出 128 億元，2026 前三季
累計資本支出達 346 億元，預估 2027 年資本支出介於 400-450 億元，投資
金額成長幅度收斂，將主要用於改良租賃廠房及電力升級。管理層表示 AI
擴廠計畫為配合 CSP 客戶未來 12-24 個月的需求規劃，且伺服器屬於客製
化接單生產(Build-to-Order)，非承擔庫存風險的標準品倍或生產，資本支出
主要投入的項目為廠房電力設施及高階冷卻系統等通用的基礎設施，因此
縱然單一客戶或是特定世代的 AI 伺服器平台需求放緩，亦可無縫轉換產能，
較不會出現過去供過於求時，閒置產能導致的折舊與維護費用等沉沒成本，
營運風險相對較小。 
2027 年伺服器展望不變：美國期中選舉後，市場對於限制資料中心相關法
案的擔憂升溫，然而廣達表示目前客戶方面並未調整訂單。廣達的客戶以五
大 CSP 為主，Neocloud 的占比不高，就現階段來看主要 CSP 的資本支出尚
未放緩，我們預估 2027 年 AI 伺服器展望將維持不變。3Q26 AI 伺服器出貨
量的下滑主要原因為新舊平台交替的陣痛期，預估 4Q26 伺服器出貨量仍將
下滑，但因 Vera Rubin 相較 GB300 近乎倍增的 ASP，AI 伺服器營收將維持
季增。此外，目前在記憶體、PCB 及 CPU 缺貨問題對於消費性產品及通用
型伺服器影響較大，但由於廣達至 2027 年的訂單人以五大 CSP 為主，且
CSP 巨頭鎖料能力較強，因此 AI 伺服器的長短料問題相較之下較輕微，我
們預期 1Q27 Vera Rubin將可如先前預期放量，帶動 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收
高雙位數增長。通用型伺服器部分，受惠 AI Agent 的應用落地帶動通用型
伺服器需求增長，預估 4Q26 通用型伺服器將持續季增。考慮到 AI Agent 的
需求持續成長，預期 2027 年通用型伺服器除 Share gain 外，亦受惠整體市
場需求提升帶來的成長，全年營收呈雙位數年增。 
管理層預期 2027 年筆電市場持續低迷：管理層預期 2027 年筆電需求將持
續受料件漲價及缺料等問題壓抑，導致 2027 年筆電出貨量下滑高個位數至
低雙位數左右，考慮到目前筆電營收占比已不足 15%，因此我們認為 2027
年合併營收將受伺服器的強勁成長帶動而年增。 
預估 4Q26 營收 1.19 兆元，稅後 EPS 7.19 元：預期 4Q26 筆電市場仍舊低
迷，AI 伺服器處於世代交替的轉換期，出貨量將持續下滑，但受 ASP 提升
影響而帶動 AI 伺服器營收季增，此外通用型伺服器出貨亦維持強勁。綜上

### Page 4

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 12 日

4 群益投顧 
所述，群益預估 4Q26 營收 1.19 兆元，QoQ+7.0%：毛利率 4.62%， QoQ-
0.13ppts，毛利續持續下滑主要受到 AI 伺服器 ASP 提升帶來的稀釋，以及
筆電出貨量下滑造成的規模經濟效果衰減所影響；營業利益 331.68 億元，
QoQ+3.0%，營益率 2.78%；歸屬母公司淨利 288.35 億元，QoQ+1.45%；稅
後 EPS 7.02 元。 
預估 2027 年營收 5.99 兆元，稅後 EPS 30.85 元：群益預估 2027 年合併營
收 5.99 兆元，YoY+44.1%；毛利率 4.18%，YoY-0.6ppts；營業利益 1,490.08
億元，YoY+21.5%，營益率 2.49%， YoY-0.5ppts；歸屬母公司淨利 1,266.99
億元，YoY+18.3%，稅後 EPS 30.85 元。 
投資建議：廣達目前未見主要 CSP 客戶調整 AI 伺服器訂單，且考慮到 CSP
對於記憶體、CPU 和 PCB 等關鍵零組件的鎖料能力，我們預期 Vera Rubin
將於如原先預期於 1Q27 放量，帶動 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收維持高雙位數成
長。通用型伺服器則受惠於 AI Agent 帶動運算需求及市占提升，預估全年
營收呈雙位數年增。預期伺服器強勁的成長將可抵銷筆電市場的低迷，帶動
2027 年營收年增，惟 Vera Rubin 的高單價將使毛利率及營益率承壓，但營
業利益亦將仍持續增長，整體營運展望維持正向，然而考慮到近期市場對於
資料中心建置的疑慮升溫而可能抑制評價，因此我們給予廣達 Trading Buy
的投資建議，目標價 324。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Realtek Semiconductor  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Realtek remains relatively resilient despite sluggish PC demand, supported by its
diversified product portfolio and platform agnostic strategy. Near-term customer
inventory adjustments appear to reflect shipment timing rather than underlying
demand weakness. Meanwhile, elevated legacy DRAM prices continue to pressure
device BOM costs, potentially delaying broader adoption of edge Al and other

consumer applications
Looking ahead, edge server and edge Al represent structural growth opportunities,
supported by ongoing development in high-speed SerDes and integrated Al
solutions. Realtek also sees opportunities to strengthen its position in higher-end TV
SoCs amid competition. A normalization in memory prices could provide an
additional catalyst for downstream demand recovery.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Shenzhen Goodix Technology
Co Ltd (603160.SS) From To
Price Target Rmb58.00 Rmb40.00
Higher memory costs are squeezing handset makers’ budgets for
displays, touch controllers and sensors. We expect weak
Android volumes to limit DDIC suppliers’ ability to pass on
higher wafer and packaging costs, even where new OLED
programs lift content per phone.
Android bears most of the decline. Our US hardware team (2026 AlphaWise

Smartphone Survey: Another Down Year That's Not Yet Priced In) expects 2027
smartphone shipments to fall 5%, versus 4% growth previously ( Exhibit 2 ). The
team's bear case is a global decline of 11%. The base case has Android down about
9% to 768mn units and iPhone up 7% to 270mn. Across the US, China, the UK and
Germany, 41% of owners were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months, down
2ppt ( Exhibit 3 ). The survey implies a replacement cycle of 4.4 years in 2027, up

from 4.2. We expect Android supply chains to face the greatest pressure in 2027:
falling shipments, continued de-specing, and margin compression leave Android
OEMs and suppliers more exposed as brands attempt to offset higher component
costs through a combination of price increases and bill of materials (BOM)
reductions.
China's replacement demand is weakening. We expect a 7% decline in China
smartphone shipments in 2027, following an estimated 12% decline in 2026. Among

China survey respondents, 48% were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months,
down from 49% last year. When asked why they chose their current phone, 17%
cited built-in AI, down from 22%, while 35% cited overall device quality, up from
32% ( Exhibit 4 ).
Brand switching favors Apple in China ( Exhibit 6 ). Among respondents in China,
Apple had the strongest net switching intention at +28% of its installed base. The
corresponding rates for Xiaomi, Oppo, Vivo and Honor ranged from -21% to -31%. A

shift in purchases toward Apple would put further further pressure on component
demand at these four Android brands.
Stock implications: Egis: Rating downgraded to UW and price target lowered from
NT$105 to NT$66; we estimate wider losses in 2026, lower our 2027 EPS estimate
25% and introduce 2028 estimates. Himax: Remain EW, price target down from US
$17.4 to US$9.2, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down 17%/17%/33%. Goodix: Remain
UW, PT down from Rmb58 to Rmb40, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Himax: Earnings Revision and Quarterly Financials
We lower 2026/27/28 EPS estimates 17%/17%/33%: We incorporate 2Q-3Q26 actual
results. We see continued weakness in the consumer market (especially smartphones) due
to elevated component costs and weak demand. Increasing material and foundry prices
might also affect Himax's margins, as we believe it is unlikely to pass through to clients.
However, we expect 2027 to see more contributions from OLED DDIC, OLED T-con

shipments and WiseEye opportunities, which should help revenue and gross margin.
Continued OLED shipments for a mainstream model and inventory builds for new
premium phones lifted combined LCD and OLED handset IC sales in 3Q26. Management
expects smartphone driver sales to decline Q/Q in 4Q26. Its LCD touch-and-display drivers
serve the entry and mid-range segments, in which pressure to cut component costs is
greatest.

Himax: Valuation Methodology
We remain EW and lower our price target to US$9.2: This factors in our lower
expectations for 2026-28 earnings. Our beta assumption is raised from 1.3 to 1.5 (leading
to an increased cost of equity from 9.9% to 11.0%); the increase in beta reflects the higher
uncertainties around end market demand and component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure.
Our terminal growth rate assumption is cut from 3.4% to 2.0%; the new terminal growth

rate is now more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed
economies over the longer term. Other residual income assumptions are unchanged,
including risk-free rate of 2%, risk premium of 6%, intermediate growth rate of 8.0%.  We
lower our bull/bear-case values from US$26.0/8.7 to US$13.7/4.6.

Base case, derived from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture the
company's long-term value. We assume a cost of equity of 11.0% (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk
premium 6.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 8.0% and a terminal growth rate of 2.0%, in
line with our assumptions for Greater China DDIC peers. We assume a payout ratio of 50%.

market.

10
Goodix: Estimate Revisions
We  lower our 2026/27/28 earnings estimates 14%/18%/5%. We factor in 2-3Q26 actual
results, and reduce our earnings forecasts to reflect weak smartphone end market
demand and cost pressures.  Smartphones accounted for 62.6% of 1H26 sales, and weaker
demand and chip pricing weighed on 3Q revenue. Xiaomi’s 18 Pro series uses two Goodix
touch controllers and its ultrasonic fingerprint sensor. Wider adoption of integrated OLED

touch and display drivers (TDDI) would displace standalone touch controllers. Goodix’s
own OLED TDDI was still in internal testing in October.

Goodix: Valuation Methodology
We maintain our UW rating. We lower our price target to Rmb40 from Rmb58: This is
based on a residual income model using our base-case forecasts. The lower price target
reflects our lower earnings estimate. We also lower our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.9% to 2.5%. The new terminal growth rate is now more in line with the macro
economic growth rate of global economies over the longer term.

Other key RI assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 8.4%, an
intermediate growth rate of 16.5%. We lower our bull-case value to Rmb55 from Rmb80
and our bear-case value to Rmb21 from Rmb31.

Egis: Valuation methodology
We downgrade our rating to UW and lower our price target from NT$105 to NT$66:
This is our base-case scenario value derived from a residual income model. This reflects
the changes in our 2026-27 earnings estimates. We roll forward our valuation date from
2025 to 2026.
Changes in our RI assumptions include:
• Cost of equity is raised to 11.0% from 9.5% (beta raised from 1.25 to 1.50). The
increase in beta reflects the higher uncertainties around end market demand and

component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure
• Intermediate growth rate cut from 14.8% to 8.0%. The decrease in intermediate
growth rate reflects our more moderate view on the end market demand recovery
and new products ramp up schedule
• Terminal growth rate cut from 5.0% to 2.0%. The new terminal growth rate is now
more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed economies
over the longer term
Other key RI assumptions remain unchanged:

• Risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%
• Payout ratio of 40%
We lower our bull-case value from  NT$143 to NT$95 and bear-case value from NT$49 to
NT$30, implying 24x and 7x 2027 EPS, respectively, on our estimates.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

研 究 報 告                
旺矽(6223.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
探針卡需求強勁挹注短期營運動能強勁、CPO 設備進度如期  
 投資評價與建議 
目前探針卡廠商評價普遍修正至20~35倍，尤其以短期營運相對穩健之廠商
(如精測、旺矽)仍相對有撐，而我們觀察公司探針卡需求強勁支撐短期營運動
能強勁，並重申CPO設備驗證進度如期、客戶數持續增加，故仍看好評價仍有
望回到合理區間之中上緣。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26營收有望雙位數季增：公司預估4Q26營收有望季增5~10%、毛利率
維持近3Q26之高檔水準，觀察探針卡BB Ratio均維持高檔水準，且擴產
計畫不變。基於公司10月營收達22.3億元創下新高、財測⼀向較為保守，
預估營收有望季增12.7%至66.6億元，毛利率61.8%，稅後EPS 21.82元。 
積極擴增MEMS產能、重申CPO客戶需求與進度如期：展望2027年：
(1)VPC和MEMS明年底有望擴至250萬針、600萬針，擴充幅度仍具有上
修空間，當前VPC主要客戶未有升級至MEMS規劃，至於MEMS主要貢獻
則來自於美系客戶伺服器CPU；(2)insertion 2客戶將於12月通知驗證結
果，1H27有望獲得量產機訂單，至於insertion 3已如期出貨工程機給3間
客戶，並預期1H27將陸續獲得量產機訂單，惟公司預估CPO設備營收顯
著貢獻時間點將落在2028年，故我們同步調整營收、獲利。 
 投資風險 
CPO進展不如預期、探針卡擴產針數下修。

### Page 2

| 營運概況 
3Q26獲利優於預期：3Q26營收季增12.8%至59億元，連續七季創下歷史新高，毛利率61.7%，稅後EPS 19.18元，
其中營收、獲利均優於預期，推估主因為探針卡產品組合改善，加上於2Q26進行之探針卡報價調整完整貢獻3Q
營收(包含CPC漲價20%、取消VPC和MEMS 10%價格優惠)，另公司營業費用管控得宜也同步挹注獲利。 
估4Q26營收有望雙位數季增：公司預估4Q26營收有望季增5~10%、毛利率有望維持近3Q26之高檔水準，我們
推估動能包含：(1)維持VPC、MEMS月產能今年底分別擴至200萬針、350萬針之進展；(2)訂單出貨則觀察探針卡
BB Ratio均維持高檔水準，其中CPC今年前10個月維持滿載、目前BB Ratio接近1，至於MEMS、VPC則超過1，
考量公司探針卡交期已明顯延⻑至4~6個月，隱含本季探針卡營收仍有望持續提升。基於公司10月營收達22.3億
元創下新高、財測⼀向較為保守，預估本季營收有望季增12.7%至66.6億元，毛利率61.8%，稅後EPS 21.82元。 
積極擴增MEMS產能、重申CPO客戶需求與進度如期：展望2027年，我們觀察：(1)探針卡中，明年CPC月產能維
持50萬針、VPC和MEMS明年底則有望擴至250萬針、600萬針，產能擴充幅度具有上修空間，且強調2028年維持
積極擴產策略，訂單貢獻中，VPC主要客戶近年未有升級至MEMS探針卡之規劃，預期該客戶AI ASIC訂單將支撐
明年VPC需求，至於MEMS明年主要貢獻則來自於美系客戶伺服器CPU，且公司持續爭取潛在客戶AI ASIC訂單，
另規劃於2H27量產之PCB廠有望挹注探針卡成本優化；(2)CPO設備中，insertion 2如我們先前所提，於3Q26底
～4Q26初送樣至客戶端進行驗證，公司預估客戶將於12月通知驗證結果，根據目前客戶回饋正向，預期1H27有
望獲得量產機訂單，此外近期有另⼀客戶與公司接洽中(公司預估1H27獲得驗證結果)，至於insertion 3則已如期
出貨工程機給3間客戶，也將陸續交機給另3間客戶，預計1H27將陸續獲得insertion 3量產機訂單，惟考量接單至
出貨、裝機驗收之時間較⻑，公司認為CPO相關設備顯著營收貢獻時間點將落在2028年，我們則同步下修明年相
關設備之營收認列數量。綜上所述，公司預估明年營收有望年增至少50%、毛利率維持在60%以上；我們則預估
明年營收有望年增58.6%至344.6億元，較先前下修主要反映部分CPO設備遞延至2028年認列營收，毛利率63.2%，
稅後EPS 117.77元。

Document 192: 6223旺矽 群益

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-23

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 23 日

1 群益投顧 
旺矽(6223 TT)AI 晶片測試 2027 續旺。旺矽估 2027 年營收成長 50%以上，毛利率維持 60%以上；
2028 年 CPO 設備有望量產，將帶動評價提升，給 Trading Buy 的投資建議。

研 究 員 ： 莊 禮 誠   
dan.chuang@cim.capital.com.tw

旺矽 3Q26 毛 利 率 季 增 ：  
旺矽 3Q26 毛 利 率 達 61.7％ ， 較 2Q26 的 58.4％提升 3.3 個 pts， 單 季 稅 後 純
益 18.80 億 元 ， 季 增 23％ 、 年 增 114％ ， 每 股 稅 後 純 益 19.18 元 ， 顯 示 高 階
產 品 需 求 放 量 ， 帶 動 獲 利 能 力 顯 著 改 善 。 旺 矽 3Q26 毛 利 率 突 破 60%， 主 要
受惠 AI 及 HPC 相 關 產 品 出 貨 比 重 提 高 。  
旺 矽 估 4Q26 營 收 季 增 ：  
AI 及 高 效 能 運 算 (HPC)晶 片 測 試 需 求 持 續 升 溫，帶 動 測 試 介 面 大 廠 旺 矽 營 運
動 能 走 強。隨 先 進 製 程 晶 片 複 雜 度 提 高、測 試 接 點 數 持 續 增 加，旺 矽 高 階 探
針 卡 需 求 維 持 強 勁，旺矽估 4Q26 營 收 可 望 季 增 5％至 10％，4Q26、2027 全
年 毛 利 率 有 機 會 維 持 60%以 上 水 準 ， 營 運 成 長 動 能 延 續 至 2027 年。 
旺 矽 估 4Q26 毛 利 率 與 3Q26 相當：  
隨 AI 晶 片 持 朝 更 高 運 算 效 能 、 更 大 晶 片 面 積 及 先 進 封 裝 架 構 發 展 ， 測 試 接
點 數 同 步 攀 升，對 探 針 卡 精 密 度、可 靠 度 及 測 試 效 率 的 要 求 也 愈 來 愈 高。旺
矽 憑 藉 高 階 測 試 技 術 及 客 戶 認 證 優 勢 ， 有 利 擴 大 先 進 晶 片 測 試 市 場 布 局 。  
值得注意的是，隨高階探針卡技術門檻提高，相關產品製造成本也同步增
加，旺 矽 具 備 將 部 分 成 本 轉 嫁 客 戶 的 能 力，有 助 降 低 成 本 上 升 對 毛 利 率 的 衝
擊 。 加 上 高 附 加 價 值 產 品 出 貨 增 加 ， 4Q26 毛 利 率 仍 有 機 會 維 持 在 60%出頭
水準。 
CPO 設 備 有 望 於 2027 年 拿 到 訂 單 ， 2028 年 貢 獻 顯著營收：  
在 新 技 術 布 局 方 面 ， 共 同 封 裝 光 學 (CPO) 被 視 為 旺 矽 下 一 階 段 重 要 成 長 機
會。隨 AI 資 料 中 心 對 高 速 傳 輸 及 低 功 耗 需 求 提 升，光 電 整 合 技 術 逐 步 導 入，
將 進 一 步 提 高 相 關 晶 片 與 模 組 測 試 複 雜 度，為 測 試 介 面 供 應 商 創 造 新 商 機。 
目前旺矽 CPO 相 關 測 試 方 案 持 續 推 進 客 戶 驗 證 ， 其 中 第 三 階 段 (Insertion 3)
已 向 多 家 客 戶 交 付 工 程 測 試 設 備 ， 預 期 1H27 有 機 會 陸 續 取 得 量 產 設 備 訂
單；第 二 階 段 (Insertion 2) 預計 12/2026 取 得 首 家 客 戶 認 證 結 果，後 續 仍 須 視
客 戶 驗 證 及 導 入 進 度 而 定 。  
旺 矽 持 續 擴 充 探 針 卡 產 能 ：  
此 外 ， 旺 矽 2027 年 擴 產 規 劃 也 是 市 場 關 注 焦 點 。 隨 AI 及 HPC 需 求 延 續 ，
高階探針卡產能配置將攸關後續訂單承接能力，公司預期在年度預算規劃
完成後，2026 年 底 前 可 望 進 一 步 釐 清 2027 年 產 能 擴 充 方 向 。  
投 資 建 議 ：  
旺矽估 2027 年 營 收 成 長 50%以 上 ， 毛 利 率 維 持 60%以 上 ； 2028 年 CPO 設
備 有 望 量 產 ， 將 帶 動 評 價 提 升 ， 給 Trading Buy 的 投 資 建 議 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】  
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 ≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <15%

中立(Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等  
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整  
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】  
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為 四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等 係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。   
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」 及 「 區 間 操 作 」 均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。  
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ：  
1. 達 目 標 價 。  
2. 雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。  
3. 推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】 
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3Q26 영업이익 1.8조, 핵심 하락 원인은 인센티브 
환율효과는 전분기 대비 -5,330억 작용했으나, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비의 급감 
(6,200억, -9,250억 qoq)으로 상쇄되었다 판단. 인센티브 비용 증가세는 -6,060억원의 영
향을 미쳤으며, 대부분 유럽지역 EV2 판매를 위한 딜러 인센티브에서 발생 추정. 
외생변수는 능력 밖의 일, EV 전략에 대해서는 장기성장에 무게 
중동 분쟁 지속, 환율 하락과 같은 외생변수의 부정적 영향을 제외한다면 단기 영업적 측면
의 실적 악재는 BEV 인센티브 지급기조의 변화가 없을 것이라는 점. 중장기 글로벌 BEV 
시장의 입지 강화를 단기 전사 수익성보다 중시하는 전략을 채택. 
마찬가지로, 4분기가 환율 효과의 본격화 
3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (QTD 1,338원) 효과로 환관련 
감익 본격화 예상. 즉, 판매보증비의 정상화 (qoq 반등)로 인해 수익성 추가 악화 가능성 존
재. 완성차 판매량의 경우 경쟁사 대비 견조했던 2Q-3Q26의 기조를 유지할 것이라 예상. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 129,000원으로 하향 
목표주가 하향 근거는 산정 기준을 FY27F로 변경함과 동시에 FY27F 자동차사업 NOPAT 
예상치 하향과 현대모비스 지분가치 하락. 밸류에이션상 저점에 도달했다는 의견은 FY27F 
EPS의 yoy 하락으로 설득력 약화. 중장기 BEV 시장 입지 강화에 대한 밸류에이션 프리미
엄 반영은 글로벌 전동화 속도에 달려 있음. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) 외생변수의 개선, 2) 신
차 출시를 통한 상품성 강화, 3) BEV 시장 점유율 확대 이후 점진적 인센티브 축소, 4) 20조
원 수준의 순현금 기반 발생 가능한 사업적 결정.

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기, 물량 감소에도 확인된 이익 방어력 
현대차의 3Q26 매출액은 45조 1,100억원(YoY -3.4%), 영업이익은 2조 6,770억원
(YoY +5.5%), OPM 5.9%(YoY +0.5%p)를 기록했다. 국내 파업 및 영업일수 감소 영
향으로 글로벌 도매 판매량은 92.1만대(YoY -11.3%)까지 감소했으나, HEV 중심의 
고부가 차종 Mix 개선, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비(YoY -1.2조원) 감소 효과
로 수익성은 오히려 개선됐다. 특히 HEV 판매 비중은 18.2%(YoY +2.7%p)까지 상
승했다. 반면 EV 판매는 전년 대비 감소세가 이어지며 지역별 수요 둔화와 경쟁 심
화 영향이 지속됐다. 
연간 눈높이는 낮아졌지만, 믹스 개선은 지속 
현대차는 글로벌 수요 둔화와 생산 차질을 반영해 2026년 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 
기존 415.8만대에서 400만대로, 영업이익률 가이던스를 기존 6.3~7.3%에서 
5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정했다. 다만 북미를 중심으로 HEV 판매가 견조하게 유지되고 
있으며, 하반기 주요 신차 투입과 전동화 라인업 확대를 통해 믹스 개선 기조는 이
어질 전망이다. 중장기적으로는 로보택시 공급 확대와 Atlas 상용화 등 Physical AI 
사업도 계획대로 진행 중으로, 단기 판매 성장 둔화에도 자동차 본업의 수익성 방어
와 신사업 모멘텀은 유효한 것으로 판단한다. 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 460,000원 하향 
글로벌 완성차 업체들의 수익성 부담은 확대되고 있다. 3분기 자동차 부문 영업이익
률은 Mercedes-Benz 2.5%, Stellantis 0.7%, Volvo -5.6%, VW -7.8%를 기록했다. 
유럽 내 BEV 판매 확대에도 중국 전기차 업체들의 공격적인 해외 진출과 가격 경
쟁, 중국 시장 부진이 동시에 나타나면서 전통 OEM의 수익성 압박이 심화되고 있
다. 이러한 환경에서 현대차는 HEV 중심의 유연한 파워트레인 전략과 북미 중심의 
Mix 개선을 통해 5% 후반의 수익성을 유지하고 있다는 점이 상대적으로 부각된다.

목표주가 (하향) 460,000원

Topics  Regulatory reform in India's insurance sector

Over the past quarter century, the Indian government has pursued the liberalization of the country's insurance market, 
with the aim of bringing stability to people's living standards and strengthening financial intermediation functions. 
Recently, however, it has begun to reverse course, tightening rules in order to better protect consumers.

■ India's insurance market has grown rapidly with deregulation 
The Indian government has long pursued 
the development of the country's insurance 
market, with the aim of bringing stability to 
people's living standards and strengthening 
financial intermediation functions. This 
stance can be attributed to two background 
factors:  
The first is that India's social security 
system remains underdeveloped. As a result, 
enrollment in health and life insurance can 
provide a vital safety net for mitigating the 
risk of sharp declines in household income 
due to unexpected illnesses or accidents.  
The second is that the sustainable 
development of the Indian economy requires 
the accumulation of long-term and stable 
domestic capital, separate from short-term 
bank deposits, in order to finance the 
development of infrastructure and the 
nurturing of industry. The hope has been that 
the enormous sums of money collected and 
managed by insurance companies can serve 
as a reliable source for this long-term 
financing.  
These factors have led the Indian 
government to walk a path of gradual 
deregulation over the past quarter century. 
The process began with the liberalization of 
market entry by private and foreign 
companies in 2000, which was followed by 
a lifting of the ban on "bancassurance" (the 
sale of insurance through bank branches) in 
2002, the raising of the ceiling on foreign 
ownership, and other reforms.  
This series of deregulatory measures 
spurred the entry of numerous private and foreign 
firms, causing market competition to intensify. Several 
Japanese insurers, including Nippon Life and the Tokio 
Marine Group, also entered the market, helping to drive 
investment in India.  
Besides deregulation related to market entry and 
sales, other factors also contributed to the rapid growth 
of India's insurance market, namely the expansion of 
the middle class, improvements in household financial 
literacy, and the digitalization of insurance services. 
Premium income grew at a pace exceeding economic 
growth, and in recent years insurance companies' assets 
under management have reached a level equivalent to 
Year Event
Establishment of the IRDAI (Insurance Regulatory
and Development Authority of India)
Acceptance of market entry applications from
domestic private firms and foreign entities
(foreign equity cap set at 26%)
2002
Authorization of bancassurance (sale of insurance
through banks or bank-owned distribution
channels)
2015 Increase in foreign equity cap from 26% to 49%
2016
Introduction of a post-facto notification system
for the sale of new insurance products (replacing
the previous prior-approval system)
2021 Increase in foreign equity cap from 49% to 74%
2022 Rationalization of regulations regarding online and diverse insurance distribution channels
2023
Removal of caps on commissions paid by
insurance companies to agents and
intermediaries
2025 Increase in foreign equity cap from 74% to 100%
2026
IRDAI releases a public consultation paper on
major reforms to the insurance distribution
system

### Page 4

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  November 2026, No.308

The Japan Research Institute, Limited  
Economics Department

3 
incentives to sell products and inappropriate sales practices. This should shore up the credibility of the 
insurance market and the foundation for its sustainable development. 
On the minus side, there are worries about side effects arising from the transition. As rules on selling 
become stricter, insurance companies and the financial institutions acting as insurance agents will inevitably 
see some temporary moderation in the pace of their earnings growth. Moreover, given that insurance 
companies and financial institutions are major investors in India's financial markets, tighter regulation is 
likely to exert downward pressure on equity and bond prices. 
These changes in the insurance market provide an important perspective for assessing the future 
trajectory of the Indian economy. They illustrate that rapid market expansion driven by liberalization can 
create a variety of problems. And this dynamic is not limited to the insurance sector, as it can also occur 
across many other industries. At the same time, the redesign and recalibration of regulations in response to 
such developments can provide a foundation for a new stage of growth. The new insurance regulations 
could constitute an early example of this phenomenon, so will be closely watched to see what changes they 
bring in the years ahead.  
  (Shotaro Kumagai)

### Page 5

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  November 2026, No.308

The Japan Research Institute, Limited  
Economics Department

4 
Topics  Talent travel curbs may weigh on China’s growth

In September, the Chinese government introduced new exit-entry rules. Indicative of an effort to retain talent in order 
to prevent technology leakage, the rules could narrow opportunities for China to absorb overseas knowledge and weigh 
on its economic growth over the medium to long term.

■ Scope of overseas travel restrictions expanded 
On September 15, the Chinese 
government brought in new rules 
specifying conditions and screening 
procedures for entry and exit. The new 
rules explicitly state that the Ministry of 
Commerce or other departments may 
prohibit Chinese citizens from leaving the 
country if they violate export-control or 
technology import/export regulations and 
are deemed likely to threaten national 
industrial or technological security.  
Under China's Exit and Entry 
Administration Law, which came into 
effect in 2013, defendants and suspects in 
criminal cases, as well as persons involved 
in unsettled civil cases whom courts have 
prohibited from leaving the country, had 
already been subject to exit restrictions. In 
addition, Chinese citizens could be denied 
permission to leave the country if the 
competent departments determined that 
their departure might endanger national 
security or interests. Aside from formal exit 
bans, personnel who handle state secrets 
and officials in the public sector have long 
been subject to controls such as centralized 
passport management and the need to 
obtain approval in advance of overseas travel. What the new rules effectively do is make it clear that exit 
can also be restricted when there are issues from the standpoint of economic security. 
Against this backdrop, it was reported at the end of September that overseas travel restrictions already 
imposed on key personnel in the AI and semiconductor sectors had been expanded to include their spouses, 
children, and other family members. According to these reports, these key personnel include prominent AI 
startup founders, senior executives at major private enterprises, and core R&D talent. Although the measure 
does not constitute a blanket travel ban, affected individuals and their families are reportedly required to 
obtain government approval for exit even in the case of short private trips or business travel. 
This regulatory tightening is thought to be related to the technological rivalry between the U.S. and China. 
As competition surrounding critical technologies such as generative AI intensifies, the Chinese government 
appears to view not only technologies themselves, but also the researchers and engineers who create them, 
as strategic assets. By widening travel curbs to encompass family members, the authorities are likely 
seeking to prevent overseas emigration by individuals or the relocation of R&D operations abroad. A 
noteworthy aspect of the recent reports is that the controls apparently now extend beyond the public sector 
to key personnel at private enterprises, as well as their families. Restrictions like these on talent mobility 
could undermine the mechanisms of technology absorption and productivity improvement that have 
hitherto facilitated China's growth.

■ China's productivity gains have been supported by market opening and technology 
absorption 
Productivity exerts a substantial influence on 
economic growth. Total factor productivity (TFP), an 
indicator of an economy's overall production 
efficiency, provides an estimate of efficiency in 
production that cannot be explained solely in terms of 
inputs of capital and labor, and reflects factors such as 
levels of technology and efficiency of resource 
allocation. 
China's TFP has been rising for many years. In 
addition to the transition to a market economy and the 
growth of private enterprises since the country adopted 
the policy of reform and opening up, Chinese 
productivity has benefitted from the absorption of 
foreign technology and managerial know‑how through 
trade and direct investment. A labor shift from 
agriculture to manufacturing and other sectors, along 
with competition among firms and the weeding out of 
weaker players, has also helped deliver productivity 
gains across the economy.  
Nevertheless, China's TFP remains low relative to 
other major economies. According to estimates from 
Penn World Table, China's TFP in 2023 was less than 
half that of the U.S., and it was also below the levels not 
only of Germany and other major European economies 
and of South Korea and Japan, but of Thailand and 
Malaysia, too. Factors behind this include inefficient 
resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced 
technologies and management practices. Preferential 
financial and institutional treatment for state‑owned 
enterprises, regional protectionism, and mechanisms 
that impede market entry and exit have distorted the 
allocation of resources, preventing capital and labor 
from being fully utilized by more productive firms and sectors. Moreover, although China has achieved 
strong competitiveness in certain advanced industries, such as electric vehicles and semiconductors, the 
leading-edge technologies and management practices developed in these sectors have not sufficiently 
diffused to the service sector and small and medium‑sized enterprises, which form the foundation of the 
economy, and this has constrained overall productivity growth.  
■ Moves to contain technology could hinder growth over the medium to long term 
Efforts to retain talent in order to prevent technology leakage could further delay China's productivity 
catch‑up with advanced economies. While restrictions on overseas travel by elite professionals may help 
with the retention of domestic R&D talent and protection of current technological advantages, they could 
also reduce opportunities to absorb overseas knowledge and to jointly develop new technologies with 
foreign partners. The transfer of technology and know‑how does not occur solely through the sharing of 
"explicit knowledge," in the form of academic papers, technical blueprints, and so on. "Tacit knowledge," 
which is transmitted through face‑to‑face discussion, collaborative work, and the acquisition of experience 
in production settings, is also crucial. If travel controls delay or prevent participation in international 
conferences, joint research projects, and technology exchanges with overseas companies, opportunities to 
absorb such knowledge may be lost, slowing technological innovation and improvements in production 
processes. Furthermore, if obtaining travel approval becomes more time‑consuming or less predictable, the 
overseas assignment or international deployment of technical personnel could also be obstructed. 
The new rules also cover the entry of foreign nationals. If uncertainty surrounding the administration of 
entry and exit controls increases, it could create obstacles to attracting and retaining highly skilled talent 
from abroad. At a time when inefficient resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced knowledge 
are already weighing on productivity growth in China, curbs on people-to- people exchange could further

impede both the absorption and creation of knowledge, as well as its practical application. Although the 
magnitude of the impact will depend on the scope of application and actual implementation on the ground, 
restrictions on talent exchanges and overseas travel that reflect excessive emphasis on technology 
protection could slow China's TFP growth over the medium to long term and act as a headwind to the 
Chinese economy by lowering its potential growth rate.

1. So far in 2026, global growth has been remarkably unremarkable.  Both the world 
as a whole and the G3 economies are growing in line with our estimates of potential 
GDP.  Our interpretation is that fiscal easing and the positive impulse from the AI 
boom have offset the negative impulse from the war in Iran and the lingering drag 
from higher US tariffs.

2. Our standing global GDP forecasts project more of the same in 2027, as lower 
energy prices offset fading tailwinds from fiscal policy and AI.  But the balance of 
risks is on the downside.  The most immediate concern is that, far from subsiding, 
energy prices have again climbed sharply on fears of renewed Middle East escalation.  
The prices of gasoline, diesel, jet fuel, and European/Asian natural gas are now near 
or even above their March/April highs, with likely negative effects on consumers and 
energy-importing economies more broadly.

3. Although we don’t expect significant fiscal policy changes in 2027, the consequences 
of large-scale deficits—coupled with AI-linked borrowing and central bank hikes—have 
started to show up more clearly in higher longer-term interest rates.  The moves have 
been mostly orderly, but the increase since early 2026 averages a sizable 100bp across 
DM yield curves.  If sustained, we estimate that this increase will subtract a cumulative 
0.6% from GDP via weakness in housing, consumer durables, and business investment.  
(Only part of the drag is incorporated in our standing forecasts because we expect bond 
yields to pull back as central banks deliver fewer hikes than priced in markets.)

Exhibit 2: Oil Price Risks Are Skewed to the Upside

4. The growth impulse from the AI boom was set to peak in late 2026 or early 2027 even 
before concerns around the sustainability of the AI investment boom intensified in 
recent weeks.  Although AI investment is still rising rapidly, we expect the increase as a 
share of GDP to slow over the next year.  Alongside the fading boost from last year’s 
increase in US depreciation allowances, this will weigh on capital spending growth, 
especially in the US.  AI has also boosted US consumer spending growth via a positive 
wealth effect worth an estimated 0.4pp (or 0.25% of GDP) over the past year.  This 
impact is likely to decline over the next year, gradually if stocks perform in line with our 
strategists’ reasonably upbeat expectations but more sharply if AI-related stocks 
continue to decline.

5. Our own base case remains that AI investment will ultimately pay off in terms of higher 
productivity and economywide profits.  However, one key channel for this payoff is labor 
market disruption, which has negative near-term macroeconomic effects.  And while DM 
labor markets still look solid in aggregate, we are starting to see clearer signs that the AI 
impact is spreading beyond the technology sector.  Across nonmanufacturing industries, 
the share of US firms that have adopted AI in regular business functions now shows a 
statistically significant negative correlation with employment growth and a statistically 
significant positive correlation with unemployment.

6. The Fed’s recent signals—explicit from Williams/Jefferson and implicit from Warsh—
effectively rule out a hike on October 28.  But the FOMC still looks very likely to deliver 
another 25bp hike to 4-4.25% on December 9 because spot inflation remains too high.  
And while we continue to expect significant disinflation next year on the back of 
reduced impulses from tariffs, energy prices, and AI-related prices, the risks to our 
forecast are on the upside, most obviously in energy.  Higher inflation could result in

7. Like the Fed, the ECB will likely hold rates steady next week and deliver a final hike (to 
2.75%) at the December meeting.  However, the turbulence in the French bond market 
poses somewhat greater downside risks to that call than in the US.  The turbulence has 
tightened financial conditions to a degree that is likely to shave ¼-½pp from annualized 
growth in coming quarters.  And if it intensifies into December, markets might perceive a 
hike as signaling insufficient focus by the ECB on the risk of Euro area fragmentation.  
While the ECB could address this issue by coupling a hike with renewed balance sheet 
expansion, the hurdle for such a move is high, in part because purchases of French 
bonds would inevitably raise concerns about moral hazard with respect to the needed 
fiscal adjustment.

November 5.  Consistent with our view that the UK economy is stronger than widely 
perceived, GDP growth has surprised on the upside this year and the unemployment rate 
declined to 4.7% in August on a 3-month average basis (with the volatile 1-month rate 
plunging to 4.1%).  Rising inflation and the looming household energy price reset in 
January imply a significant risk of further moves, although market pricing of 4+ hikes 
(including November) still looks excessive.  We also expect hikes in Canada and New 
Zealand this week and in Australia next week.

9. Slightly faster than expected growth in Q3 has pushed up our 2026 GDP forecast for 
China to 4.6%.  But most activity indicators are much weaker.  Both property activity and 
fixed asset investment are contracting year-on-year, and while nominal retail sales 
growth picked up to +1.1% in September, large retailers (whose sales are relatively 
better measured) are still posting substantial year-on-year declines.  With domestic 
demand rising slowly at best, sustained GDP growth in the official 4.5-5% target range 
will require ongoing steep increases in the trade surplus.  But the surplus is already at 1% 
of global GDP, and further increases will eat more noticeably into output and 
employment abroad.  Ultimately, China’s trading partners may respond by imposing 
trade barriers that weigh on Chinese exports and GDP, although the measures taken so 
far have been limited.

Exhibit 8: UK Growth Forecasts Have Drifted Higher

Source: Bloomberg, Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   6
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 7

10. Our strongest global view is that central banks will deliver fewer hikes than currently 
priced.  In addition, our rates strategists flag underweight positioning, diminished 
technical risks, and risk asset pressures as reasons why the recent rally might extend.  
The market’s narrow breadth makes our equity strategists wary of momentum factor 
exposure, but they still expect the S&P 500 to resume its climb after the midterm 
election, as earnings continue to grow solidly.  Our credit strategists remain cautious on 
IG technology bonds but see scope for some near-term reversal in HY given the scale of 
the recent selloff.  Our FX strategists expect the Japanese yen to act as a hedge against 
further AI jitters, in part because such jitters would shrink US-Japan interest rate 
differentials.  And our commodity strategists recommend targeted exposures to oil 
products and European natural gas to hedge near-term escalation risks alongside 
longer-term exposures to copper, gold, and select power markets to benefit from 
structural supply/demand imbalances.

Document 413: US Economics Analyst_ 2027 US Outlook_ The End of the Inflation Shocks

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Economics Analyst_ 2027 US Outlook_ The End of the Inflation Shocks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 20

### Page 1

The main economic surprise of 2026, the energy shock from the Iran war, has not n
prevented the US economy from growing at a healthy pace but has reversed 
progress on inflation.  The key drivers of growth and inflation in 2026—the war, 
tax cuts, tariffs, and AI price pressures—should all have less impact next year.  
We expect this to leave 2027 GDP growth at an above-consensus 2.4% and the 
labor market stable, while allowing core PCE inflation to fall substantially to 2.4%. 
Growth should follow the same pattern in 2027 as in 2026: solid consumption n
growth and strong business investment growth offset by drags from housing and 
trade.  Consumption should grow a bit over 2%, driven mainly by firmer and more 
evenly balanced real income growth as inflation moderates and the boosts from 
last year’s tax cuts and wealth effects diminish.  AI drives half of our 8% forecast 
for business investment growth but contributes only modestly to measured GDP 
growth because much of the equipment is imported or undercounted in the GDP 
statistics.  Housing investment will likely contract again as poor affordability 
continues to depress demand. 
Near-potential growth should keep the labor market stable in 2027.  This year n
began with fears of a looming AI-driven layoff wave, but the layoff rate instead 
remained historically low, and the labor market settled into a slightly softer 
low-hire, low-fire form of full employment where wage growth has stabilized at a 
subdued pace of about 3.5%.  We expect this equilibrium to last for another year 
before AI displacement begins to have a larger impact in subsequent years. 
While the near-term inflation outlook remains uncertain, we are more confident n
that inflation will fall substantially by late 2027 as the effects of tariffs, the Iran 
war energy shock, and AI price pressures diminish.  We see these three forces as 
the key to the outlook because the economy is not overheated at a 
macroeconomic level or even at an industry level outside of pockets tied to the 
AI boom, and high inflation does not appear to have become entrenched in 
expectations.  As a result, inflation should moderate as long as these forces 
subside and no new shocks emerge. 
The Fed is likely to remain firmly focused on inflation in 2027.  We forecast two n
more 25bp rate hikes in December 2026 and March 2027 to a peak of 
4.25-4.5%, followed by gradual cuts back to 3.25-3.5% starting in November, the 
final meeting of 2027, once inflation has declined meaningfully.  The FOMC will 
also discuss the conclusions of the task forces next year, but we expect any

+1(212)357-2619 |

### Page 2

institutional changes to be limited.  In particular, a large reduction in the size of the
Fed’s balance sheet is unlikely, though a shift in the composition of its assets is
possible.
The most obvious risk to our forecast of sturdy growth and a long-awaited decline in n
inflation is that the inflation shocks of the last two years, which we and most
forecasters did not anticipate, could last longer, or new shocks could arise.  We also
see downside risks to growth from the war, a longer period of higher interest rates,

or earlier and more rapid job displacement from AI.  But our best guess is that there
will be fewer big policy-driven macroeconomic shocks in 2027.

### Page 3

2027 US Outlook: The End of the Inflation Shocks

The main economic surprise of 2026, the energy shock from the Iran war, has not 
prevented the US economy from growing at a healthy pace.  We expect GDP growth of 
2.5% in 2026 Q4/Q4, in line with our forecast from our 2026 Outlook but above both 
consensus expectations at the time and our forecast after the war began.  In contrast, 
the combination of the energy shock and price pressures sparked by the AI investment 
boom are likely to leave core PCE inflation at 3.2% on a Q4/Q4 basis, a disappointment 
to the more optimistic expectation for a large decline that we and consensus held at the 
start of the year.

The key drivers of growth and inflation in 2026—the war, tax cuts, tariffs, and AI price 
pressures—should all have less incremental impact in 2027.  We expect this to leave 
2027 Q4/Q4 GDP growth at 2.4% (above consensus at 2.2%) and the labor market 
stable, while allowing core PCE inflation to fall to 2.4% (in line with consensus).

### Page 4

The Growth Outlook: Solid Consumption, Strong Business Investment, 
and Weak Housing Balance Out to Near-Potential GDP Growth 
Growth should follow the same pattern in 2027 as in 2026: solid consumption growth 
and strong business investment growth offset by drags from housing and trade.  We 
expect this to balance out to overall GDP growth that is a hair above our 2.3% estimate 
of potential growth.  Relative to consensus, our growth forecast is a touch higher on 
consumer spending, much higher on business investment, and notably weaker on 
housing and net exports.

### Page 5

The resilience of the US economy this year in the face of the energy shock has been 
particularly impressive in the case of consumer spending.  Despite a boost to headline 
inflation that has left real wage growth close to zero this year, consumer spending has 
grown at a strong pace, supported by tax refunds, a positive wealth effect from the 
stock market, and a modest decline in the saving rate. 
Our consumer outlook for 2027 is less complicated: we expect 2.2% growth in consumer 
spending driven mainly by stronger real labor income growth as inflation moderates, 
with special factors playing a more limited role.  We do not expect a meaningful change 
in the saving rate, which is likely close to 5% after accounting for revised spending and 
income data that are incorporated with a lag, putting it roughly in line with what one 
would expect at a time when household wealth is high and layoff risk is low. 
Both real income and consumer spending should grow more evenly across the income 
distribution in 2027.  While we noted last year that real income was actually more in line 
with pre-pandemic trends across income quintiles than the K-shaped narrative 
suggested, in 2026 income growth has been weaker for low-income families, due in part 
to fiscal policy changes and the unequal burden of the energy shock.  Income growth 
should even out in 2027, with a boost at the bottom from lower inflation, cost-of-living 
adjustments to government transfer payments, and the likely reversal of recent cuts to 
social spending.

AI will remain the dominant driver of business investment growth in 2027.  We estimate 
that spending on AI-related hardware and intellectual property will exceed $900bn 
annualized by end-2026 (a $550bn increase from 2025) and $1.4tn by end-2027 (a 
further $500bn increase), roughly 4% of GDP. 
The midterm election results should pose only modest risks to AI investment in 2027.  
We expect newly elected Democratic governors in states that account for over a third of 
planned US data center capacity to impose moratoria on new data centers.  But these 
are unlikely to be long-lived and should only affect projects not yet permitted, leaving 
the vast majority of planned 2027 construction unaffected.

### Page 6

The impact of AI on the official economic statistics will be smaller than company 
spending data suggest, for a few reasons.  First, spending will rise less in real than in 
nominal terms because technology goods prices are likely to rise further.  Second, 
semiconductor investment is undercounted in the business investment statistics.  Third, 
most of the technology goods used for AI are imported and are therefore netted out in 
the GDP calculation. 
Despite the undercounting, AI drives half of our 8% forecast for business investment 
growth in 2027.  Its impact on GDP growth is more nuanced: we estimate it will 
contribute 0.2pp to officially measured GDP growth; 0.7pp to true GDP growth, adding 
back undercounted semiconductor investment and exports of chip design services; or 
0.6pp if we also include positive and negative spillovers such as wealth effects and 
limited crowding out of other investment.  One implication of these calculations is that 
growth in the rest of the economy—GDP growth minus the 0.2pp measured impact—is 
not as weak as often claimed.

### Page 7

Housing is likely to remain the weakest part of the economy in 2027 as very poor 
affordability continues to depress demand.  Single-family homebuilding has remained 
close to pre-pandemic levels so far even though mortgage rates are now 3.5pp higher.  
The main reason has been the housing shortage, which has meant that despite worse 
affordability, demand has remained sufficient to absorb the limited supply available 
without a large decline in home prices, which has sustained the incentive to build.  But 
with the housing shortage now less acute and margins for homebuilders normalizing, 
homebuilding is likely to become more sensitive to changes in interest rates.  Higher 
interest rates will also continue to dampen housing turnover by locking in homeowners 
who have low interest rates on their mortgages.

### Page 8

The Labor Market Outlook: A Slightly Softer Form of Full Employment 
While 2026 began with fears of a looming AI-driven layoff wave, jobless claims instead 
fell even further and the layoff rate remained historically low.  Coupled with a slow pace 
of hiring but a stabilization in job openings at a healthy level, this has left the labor 
market in a slightly softer low-hire, low-fire form of full employment.

Our slack tracker—a composite of ten measures of labor market tightness—likewise 
signals that the labor market is a touch softer than the unemployment rate alone 
suggests.  We expect the current equilibrium to persist in 2027, with job growth 
remaining healthy as the economy grows near potential. 
But companies will eventually want to use AI to reduce labor costs more meaningfully, 
and we expect faster displacement of workers from AI-exposed jobs to gradually push 
the unemployment rate higher in subsequent years.  The early evidence on how 
disruptive this will be is mixed.  The labor market already looks tougher for workers 
displaced from AI-exposed jobs, many of whom are moving into roles with similar or 
higher AI exposure that leave them vulnerable.  But young people, who have tended to 
adapt better historically, are already adjusting by shifting to college majors less exposed 
to AI displacement.

### Page 9

The convergence of our wage growth tracker and survey-based wage growth 
expectations suggests that wage growth is stabilizing at about 3.5%.  With productivity 
growth running above 2% this cycle, this implies subdued inflationary pressure from 
labor costs.  We see little reason to expect wage growth to accelerate meaningfully next 
year because the labor market is not unusually tight and workers appear to be aware 
that finding a new job is harder than usual and concerned about displacement risk from 
AI.

### Page 10

Shock, and AI Price Pressures 
The bulk of the upside surprise to inflation in 2026 came from the Iran war energy shock 
and AI price pressures, and the effects of those two forces and tariffs remain central to 
our inflation forecast for 2027.  The economy is not overheated at a macroeconomic 
level, and our bottlenecks tracker suggests it is not overheated at an industry level either 
outside of pockets tied to the AI boom.  Nor does high inflation appear to have become 
entrenched in expectations.  As a result, inflation should moderate as long as the effects 
of tariffs, the war, and AI price pressures diminish and no new shocks emerge.

The impact of earlier tariff rounds on consumer prices appears to be over, and the 
impact on year-over-year inflation should continue to shrink as the base effects from 
last year drop out.  We expect the White House to replace some of the tariffs that were 
ruled unconstitutional by the Supreme Court, but this should have only a modest impact 
because the tariff increase will likely be small and our analysis indicates that removing 
tariffs had a minimal effect on consumer prices, so restoring them should too.

The outlook for the Iran war remains uncertain, but our commodity strategists expect 
Brent oil prices to remain near $100/bbl over the next few months before easing 
gradually through 2027 to $80/bbl.  In that scenario, we estimate that two-thirds of the 
cumulative passthrough from higher commodity prices to core PCE prices is behind us, 
and that the impact on year-over-year core PCE inflation will rise from 0.5pp now to a 
peak of 0.6pp in coming months before declining to 0.1pp by the end of 2027.

The near-term outlook for AI price pressures is also quite uncertain, but our equity 
analysts expect memory price growth to slow sharply in 2027 as new production 
capacity boosts supply, and this should also restrain inflation for other consumer

The Fed Outlook: Two More Hikes in December and March, Followed by 
Cuts Starting in November 
Under our economic forecast, the FOMC would remain firmly focused on the inflation 
side of its mandate in 2027.  We expect two more 25bp rate hikes in December 2026 
and March 2027 for a total of three to a peak of 4.25-4.5%, though a third hike could 
come earlier in January or the FOMC could deliver more than three hikes if the next two 
inflation reports do not look better, as we expect.  We expect the FOMC to begin a series 
of gradual cuts back to 3.25-3.5% starting at the final meeting of 2027 in November (the 
meeting was previously scheduled for December) once inflation has declined.  Both our 
baseline and probability-weighted Fed forecasts are less hawkish than market pricing.

### Page 14

The FOMC will also consider the conclusions of the task forces next year.  We expect any 
changes to be limited.  The balance sheet task force is unlikely to lead to a large 
reduction in the size of the Fed’s balance sheet, though a shift in the composition of its 
assets toward bills is possible.  The communications task force could lead to changes to 
the Summary of Economic Projections, but we would be surprised if it were eliminated 
entirely because this would seem like a large step away from transparency.  The inflation 
frameworks task force could engage with a range of questions, from how to respond to 
inflation caused by supply shocks to what the best measure of inflation is for the Fed.  
We do not expect major policy changes to come out of the alternative data or 
productivity and jobs task forces. 
Risks to Our Forecast of Sturdy Growth and Lower Inflation in 2027 
The most obvious risk to our forecast of sturdy growth and a long-awaited decline in 
inflation is that the inflation shocks of the last two years, which we and most forecasters 
did not anticipate, could last longer, or new shocks could arise.  But our best guess is that 
there will be fewer big policy-driven macroeconomic shocks in 2027, and it would be a 
fairly high bar for, say, new tariffs or a reescalation of the war with Iran to have as large 
of an impact in rate of change terms as they did over the last two years. 
We also see several downside risks to growth.  Further, increases in oil prices would 
weigh on real income growth and consumption.  A longer period of higher interest rates 
could also weigh on growth and reignite fiscal concerns, though our strategists’ forecasts 
imply a partial reversal of the recent tightening in financial conditions in 2027.  Job 
displacement from AI could come earlier and faster than we expect.  And AI could also 
introduce new risks, such as a proliferation of cyberattacks enabled by advanced AI 
tools.  But most of these risks seem more likely to have moderate rather than 
recessionary effects.

Document 435: 「右手と左手」の化学と「株式と債券」の経済学－ノーベル化学賞から考える「知の分散投資」と日本経済の未来

Source PDF: all report pdf/「右手と左手」の化学と「株式と債券」の経済学－ノーベル化学賞から考える「知の分散投資」と日本経済の未来.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

【要旨】 
2026年のノーベル化学賞に輝いた硤合憲三氏らの研究は、ごく小さな偏りが増幅され、大きな結果に
つながることを示した。 
この研究にみられる「対称性」は、株式と債券が逆方向に動き、価格変動を補い合う「逆相関」を考
える上での興味深い比喩となる。しかし、近年は物価上昇などを背景に両者の逆相関が弱まる局面も
みられ、物価の安定と持続的な経済成長の両立が重要となっている。 
その成長力を高める原動力の一つが研究開発である。特に、成果の予測が難しい基礎研究では、多様
な分野に幅広く投資する「知の分散投資」が求められる。政府が掲げる「科学の再興」について研究
開発投資の量と質の両面から実効性を検証し、小さな発見を技術革新や経済成長につなげ、その成果
を再び研究開発に振り向ける好循環を育てたい。 
今回のノーベル賞受賞が、日本の科学技術と経済の明るい未来につながることを期待する。

1 ――ノーベル化学賞が示した「対称性」と「小さな偏り」 
12月10日のノーベル賞授賞式を控え、受賞者の研究成果や授賞理由が改めて注目される。2026年
のノーベル化学賞を受賞する硤合憲三氏ら1の研究は、右手と左手のような鏡像関係にある分子につい
て、ごくわずかな偏りが「不斉自己触媒反応」によって増幅され、一方が圧倒的に優勢になる仕組み
を明らかにした。反応によって生まれた分子自身が、同じ型の分子を生み出す触媒となる点に、この
反応の特徴がある。 
長年にわたる基礎研究が世界的に評価されたことは、日本にとって明るいニュースである。この研
究で着目された「対称性」と「小さな偏りの増幅」は、金融市場や経済の動きを考えるうえでも興味
深い。辞書で「対称」を調べると、二つのものが互いに向かい合うこと、互いに対応しながらつり合

1 アンリ・B・カガン氏（パリ・サクレー大学名誉教授）とともに受賞 
2026-11-19 
研究員 
の眼 
「右手と左手」の化学と 
「株式と債券」の経済学 
ノーベル化学賞から考える「知の分散投資」と日本経済の未来

総合政策研究部 上席研究員 新美 隆宏 
(03)3512-1803  t-niimi@nli-research.co.jp 
ニッセイ基礎研究所

### Page 2

2 ――株式と債券の「逆相関」と望ましい経済環境 
資産運用において、値動きの異なる資産を組み合わせることは、分散投資の基本的な考え方である。
現代ポートフォリオ理論を確立したマーコビッツは、資産間の相関関係に着目し、異なる資産を組み
合わせることで、ポートフォリオ全体のリスクを軽減できるとした。株式と債券は、この代表的な組
み合わせの一つである。例えば、景気が改善すると企業収益への期待から株価が上昇する一方、成長
期待の高まりなどから金利が上昇し、債券価格が下落することがある。株式と債券の価格が逆方向に
動く逆相関の関係にあれば、資産全体の価格変動を和らげる効果が期待できる。このように、互いの
価格変動を補い合うという意味では、辞書が示す「対称」の一つの意味を連想させる関係といえる。 
図表１は、国内株式と国内債券の相関係数2を、物価上昇率3や長短金利4とともに示したものである。
相関係数を見ると、1995年頃から 2023年頃まで、長期間にわたって逆相関の関係が続いてきた。し
かし、それ以前や2023年以降には順相関5となる局面も見られ、2026年９月には相関係数がプラス0.3
程度となっている。 
株式と債券の相関係数には、物価や金融政策、景気動向など、さまざまな要因が影響する。景気や
成長期待の変化が金融市場の主な変動要因である場合、株式と債券は先に示したように逆方向に動き
やすい。一方、物価上昇率の高まりによる金融引締めが警戒される局面では、金利上昇によって債券
価格が下落するとともに、企業の資金調達コストの増加などから株価が下落することがある。このよ
うな株式と債券が同時に値下がりする局面では、分散投資によるリスク軽減効果は弱まってしまう。
分散投資の観点では、株式と債券の逆相関は望ましい関係であるものの常に成り立つわけではなく、
特に物価上昇が金融市場の主要な変動要因となる場合には、この関係は崩れやすい。 
日本では、バブル崩壊後の長期にわたる金融緩和を経て、ようやく金融政策の正常化が進みつつあ
る。ただし、政策金利が上昇すること自体が、望ましい経済・金融環境の実現を意味するわけではな
い。物価の安定と持続的な経済成長を両立させることが重要である。

2 国内株式はTOPIX（配当無し）、国内債券はNOMURA-BPI（総合）。相関係数は両者の過去 60か月の月次騰落率から算出（ロ
ーリング）。計測期間は1990年1月～2026年９月。相関係数は、使用する指数や計測期間の長短などにより異なる点に留意
が必要である 
国内株式としてTOPIX（配当込み）を使うことも考えられるが、1989年からの公表のため、1990年代初頭の高金利期を含め
た相関係数を算出できない点を考慮してTOPIX（配当無し）を用いた。なお、両指数とNomura-BPI（総合）の相関係数につ
いて1994年以降を比較したところ、結果に大きな差はみられなかった。 
3 物価上昇率は総務省「消費者物価指数（全国、総合）」の前年同月比 
4 短期金利は無担保コール翌日物、長期金利は日本国債（10年） 
5 順相関とは、逆相関とは逆の状況で、二つの資産の価格が同じ方向に動く傾向があること。本稿では、株式と債券の相関
係数が正になることを示す

### Page 3

経済成長を伴わない金利上昇は、企業の資金調達コストや家計の負担を増やし、経済活動を冷え込
ませる可能性がある。これに対し、実質所得や企業収益、生産性の向上に裏付けられた健全な成長を
実現できれば、金融政策の正常化を進めるうえでも、より安定的な経済環境となる。さらに、物価が
安定し、景気や成長期待が金利変動の主な要因となる環境では、株式と債券の逆相関が成り立つこと
も期待できる。物価の安定と持続的な成長は、実体経済だけでなく、金融市場の観点からも重要なの
である。そして、日本経済の成長力を中長期的に高める原動力の一つが、研究開発を通じた技術革新
である。

3 ――基礎研究への「知の分散投資」 ～日本の研究開発投資の課題～ 
日本の研究開発の状況を確認する。図表２－１を見ると、日本の研究開発費対GDP比は、1990年代以
降は世界有数の水準を維持している。しかし、図表２－２を見ると、基礎研究費対 GDP比は 0.4％前
後で長期間にわたり横ばいとなっており、大きく増加した韓国に抜かれ、米国やフランスを下回って
いる。研究開発投資全体の規模が大きい一方、将来の技術革新の源泉となる基礎研究への投資には課
題があるのではないか。

### Page 4

長期的な視点で見れば、研究投資の伸び率に生じた小さな差も、積み重なることで大きな差となり
得る。基礎研究の充実に向けた取り組みが十分に進まなければ、将来の研究成果や技術革新につなが
る知識の蓄積にも差が生じかねない。研究開発は、基礎研究、応用研究、開発研究に大別される。基
礎研究は、新たな知識や理論を探究するものであり、その成果がいつ、どのような技術革新につなが
るかを事前に予測することは困難であり、将来の成長に向けて様々な「種」を蒔く段階といえよう。
一方、応用研究や開発研究では、研究成果の用途や実用化の可能性が次第に明らかになるため、有望
な技術や分野に資金を重点配分することの重要性が高まる。ここには、金融市場における分散投資と
通じる考え方がある。金融資産と基礎研究への投資は性質が異なるものの、将来の不確実性に備えて
投資対象を分散させるという点では共通している。金融市場では特定の資産への集中投資が運用成果
を大きく左右するように、基礎研究でも特定の分野だけに投資を集中させると、新たな発見や技術革
新の芽を見逃すおそれがある。多様な研究領域を幅広く支える、いわば「知の分散投資」が重要にな
るだろう。基礎研究で生まれた小さな発見が、応用研究や技術開発を通じて思いがけない用途を生み、
長い年月を経て大きな技術革新へと発展することもある。どの発見が将来の大きな成果につながるか
を予測できないからこそ、多様な研究を支え、可能性の芽を育てることに意味がある。基礎研究への
分散投資は、単に研究の失敗リスクを分散するだけでなく、将来の技術革新に向けた幅広い選択肢の
確保につながる。 
基礎研究の段階では幅広く種を蒔き、応用研究や開発研究では有望な成果を選んで育てていく。こ
うした研究開発の各段階に応じた「分散」と「集中」の組み合わせが、将来の新産業の創出や生産性
の向上につながり、日本経済の成長力を支えることになる。

### Page 5

5 ――小さな発見を大きな成長へ ～研究開発と経済の好循環～ 
研究開発は、技術革新や生産性の向上を通じて経済成長を支える。同時に、健全な経済成長は企業
収益や家計所得を増やし、民間の研究開発投資や税収を通じた公的な研究支援の拡充を可能にする。
経済成長と研究開発は、一方通行ではなく、互いを支え合う関係にある。こうした好循環を支えるの
が、物価の安定と持続的な経済成長、そして金融市場を通じた円滑な資金循環である。安定した経済・
金融環境のもとで資金が企業の成長投資や基礎研究に向かい、その成果が新たな産業や企業活動の発
展につながれば、次の研究開発を支える力も生まれる。小さな研究成果が次の成果を生み、やがて大
きな成長へとつながる可能性は、冒頭で紹介した「小さな偏りの増幅」を連想させる。将来に向けて
多様な研究の種を蒔き続け、その成果を経済の成長につなげる。そして、成長によって生まれた資金
を再び研究開発に振り向ける。こうした好循環を育てることが、日本経済の持続的な発展につながる
のではないだろうか。 
12月 10日のノーベル賞授賞式を迎えるにあたり、硤合憲三氏らを改めて祝福したい。長年にわた
る研究が実を結んだ今回の受賞が、次世代の研究者の新たな挑戦を後押しし、日本の科学技術と経済
の未来を明るく照らすことを期待したい。

本資料記載のデータは各種の情報源から入手・加工したものであり、その正確性と完全性を保証するものではありません。 
また、本資料は情報提供が目的であり、記載の意見や予測は、いかなる契約の締結や解約を勧誘するものではありません。

歐洲商用飛機製造龍頭空中巴士於10/28發布2Q26
財報，第二季商用飛機交付量為184架飛機比去年
同期，較去年同期大幅成長8%。由於引擎供應與部

交付870架商用飛機目標與上季相同。
